w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}
also belongs to
\mathbb{W}
. We define
\theta^{*}
s.t.
w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}=\eta\{\theta^{*}\}^{\top}\psi
.

Statement II:
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{W}

For any
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle(\gamma\mathcal{T}^{\prime}-I)\{\eta\theta^{\top}\psi-\eta\{\theta^{*}\}^{\top}\psi\}=(\gamma M^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)(\theta-\theta^{*})\cdot\eta\psi.

Thus,
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{W}
is concluded.

Statement III:
(\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}\mathbb{W}

From
Example

13
and (
13
), for
f_{2}=\theta\cdot\eta\psi
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}f_{2}=\{\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]^{-1}(\gamma M^{\prime\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]\}\theta\cdot\eta\psi

Since
(I-\gamma M^{\prime\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})
is non-singular from the assumption
1/\gamma\notin\sigma(M_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})
, for any
w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}=(\theta-\theta^{*})\cdot\eta\psi
, we can take
w^{\prime}

\displaystyle w^{\prime}=\{\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]^{-1}(\gamma M^{\prime\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]\}^{-1}(\theta-\theta^{*})\cdot\eta\psi

s.t.
w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}=\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}w^{\prime}
. This implies
(\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}\mathbb{W}
.

Statement IV: Tabular case

We use a matrix formulation following
Example

11
. W-completeness ,
\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{W}
, obviously holds. Thus, we prove adjoint w-completeness . Recall
\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]
is non-singular, and we have

\displaystyle[\mathcal{T}^{\prime}f]=(\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]^{-1}(P^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}])[f]

and

\displaystyle[\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}f]=[(\gamma\mathcal{T}^{\prime}-I)f]=(\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]^{-1}(\gamma P^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}])[f].

Thus, for given any
f\in\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}
, we can take a vector

f^{\prime}:=\{(\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}]^{-1}(\gamma P^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\psi\psi^{\top}])^{-1}[f]\}\cdot\psi

s.t.

\displaystyle(\gamma\mathcal{T}^{\prime}-I)f^{\prime}=f.

Note
\gamma P^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I
is non-singular. Therefore, the adjoint w-completeness ,
(\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}\mathbb{W}
holds.

∎

Proof of Corollary
5.5
.

First Statement

Define
J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}r]
. Then,

\displaystyle|J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}-J|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}]|=|\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}]|\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\|\|q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2},

\displaystyle|J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}-J|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}]|\leq\|(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\|\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}.

Thus, when
w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{W}_{1},C_{\xi}\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{W}_{1}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{Q}_{1}
, with
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}-J|

\displaystyle\leq|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}-J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}|+|J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}-J|

\displaystyle\leq|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}-J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}|+\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\|\|q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Err}^{\prime}_{\mathrm{mil},w}+\sqrt{C^{-1}_{\xi}\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},w}}\|q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}.

Similarly, when
q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{Q}_{2},C_{\xi}(\mathbb{W}_{1}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}\mathbb{Q}_{1}
, with
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{w}-J|

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{Err}^{\prime}_{\mathrm{mil},w}+\sqrt{C^{-1}_{\xi}\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},w}}\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}.

Second Statement

When
q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{Q}_{2},C_{\xi}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{Q}_{2}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{W}_{2}
, with
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{q}-J|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|\leq\|w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\|\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\sqrt{C^{-1}_{\xi}\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},q}}.

When
q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{Q}_{2},C_{\xi}(\mathbb{Q}_{2}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}\mathbb{W}_{2}
, with
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{q}-J|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\|\|(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\sqrt{C^{-1}_{\xi}\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},q}}.

Third Statement

We define

J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{wq}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\{r-\widehat{q}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[\widehat{q}(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})].

When we have
q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{Q}_{2},C_{\xi}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{Q}_{2}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{W}_{2}
, with
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{wq}-J|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[\{w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-w\}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|\leq\|w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-\widehat{w}\|_{2}\|\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{1}}\|w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\lesssim\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{1}}\|w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\sqrt{C^{-1}_{\xi}\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},q}}

When we have
w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{W}_{1},C_{\xi}\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{W}_{1}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{Q}_{1}
, with
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{wq}-J|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-\widehat{w})\|_{2}\|\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{1}}\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\lesssim\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\sqrt{C^{-1}_{\xi}\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},w}}

Noting

|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{wq}-J^{\mathrm{mil}}_{wq}|\lesssim\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},wq}
, the statement is proved as

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{wq}-J|\leq\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},wq}+C^{-1/2}_{\xi}\max(\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{1}}\|w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\sqrt{\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},q}},\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\sqrt{\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},w}}).

This immediately implies

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{wq}-J|\leq\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},wq}+C^{-1/2}_{\xi}\max(C_{\mathbb{W}}\sqrt{\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},q}},C_{\mathbb{Q}}\sqrt{\mathrm{Err}_{\mathrm{mil},w}}).

Regarding the statement related to adjoint completeness, the statement similarly holds.

∎

F.3
Proof of
Section

6

In the following we will frequently invoke two key lemmas. First, recall that the empirical localized Rademacher complexity of the function class is
\mathcal{G}:V\to[-c,c]
for a given set of samples
S=\{v_{i}\}
is defined as

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{n}(\eta;\mathcal{G})=\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}:\|g\|_{n}\leq\eta}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\epsilon_{i}g(v_{i})\right].

The critical radius of
\mathcal{G}
is defined as a solution to
\mathcal{R}_{n}(\eta;\mathcal{G})\leq\eta^{2}/c
.

Lemma F.1

(Theorem 14.1,
Wainwright 2019
)
.

Given a star-shaped and
b
-uniformly bounded function class
\mathcal{G}
, let
\eta_{n}
be any positive solution of the inequality
R_{s}(\eta;\mathcal{G})\leq\eta^{2}/b
. Then, for any
t\geq\eta_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle|\|g\|^{2}_{n}-\|g\|^{2}_{2}|\leq 0.5\|g\|^{2}_{2}+0.5t^{2},\forall g\in\mathcal{G}.

Consider a function class
\mathcal{F}:X\to\mathbb{R}
with loss
l:\mathbb{R}\times Z\to\mathbb{R}
.

Lemma F.2

(Lemma 11
(
Foster and
Syrgkanis 2019
)
)
.

Assume
\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\|f\|_{\infty}\leq c
and pick any
f^{*}\in\mathcal{F}
. We define
\eta
be a solution to

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{n}(\eta;\mathrm{star}(\mathcal{F}-f^{*}))\leq\eta^{2}/c.

Moreover, assume that the loss
l(\cdot,\cdot)
is
L
-Lipschitz in the first argument. Then, with
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle|(\mathbb{E}_{n}[l(f(x),z)]-\mathbb{E}_{n}[l(f^{*}(x),z)])-(\mathbb{E}[l(f(x),z)]-\mathbb{E}[l(f^{*}(x),z)])|\leq L\eta_{n}(\|f-f^{*}\|_{2}+\eta_{n}),\,\forall f\in\mathcal{F},

where
\eta_{n}=\eta+c_{0}\sqrt{\log(c_{1}/\delta)/n}
for some universal constants
c_{0},c_{1}
.

Proof of
Theorem

6.1
.

Preliminary

Define

\displaystyle\Xi(w,q)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)\{-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]],

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}(w,q)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{n}[w(s,a)\{-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]],

\displaystyle\Xi^{\lambda}(w,q)

\displaystyle=\Xi^{\lambda}(w,q)-\lambda\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w,q)

\displaystyle=\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w,q)-\lambda\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}.

From Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\forall q\in\mathbb{Q},|\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}|\leq 0.5\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}+0.5\eta^{2},

(20)

for our choice of
\eta:=\eta_{n}+c_{0}\sqrt{\log(c_{1}/\delta)/n}
noting
\eta_{n}
upper bounds the critical of
\mathcal{G}_{w1}
.

First Step

By definition of
\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}
and
w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{W}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q)\leq\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q).

(21)

From Lemma
F.2
, with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\forall q\in\mathbb{Q}:|\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)|\lesssim C_{\mathbb{W}}\{\eta\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|_{2}+\eta^{2}\}.

noting
\eta
upper-bounds the critical radius of
\mathcal{G}_{w1}
. Here, we use
l(a_{1},a_{2})(a_{1}=-q^{\prime}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}),a_{2}=w(s,a))
is
C_{\mathbb{W}}
-Lipschitz with respect to
a_{1}
noting
w(s,a)
is in
[-C_{\mathbb{W}},C_{\mathbb{W}}]
. More specifically,

\displaystyle|l(a_{1},a_{2})-l(a^{\prime}_{1},a_{2})|\leq|a_{1}-a^{\prime}_{1}|C_{\mathbb{W}}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)

\displaystyle=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)+C_{\mathbb{W}}\{\eta\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|_{2}+\eta^{2}\}-\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)+C_{\mathbb{W}}\{\eta\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|_{2}+\eta^{2}\}-0.5\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}+\lambda\eta^{2}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)+(\lambda+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}})\eta^{2}\}.

In the last line, we use a general inequality
a,b\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}(a\|f\|-b\|f\|^{2})\leq a^{2}/4b.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)

\displaystyle=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)+\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\geq\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-2\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}+\inf_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)+\lambda\|q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-2\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}+\inf_{-q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},-q)+\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-2\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}+\inf_{-q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)+\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-2\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}-\sup_{-q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)-2\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}-\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)\}.

Here, we use
\mathbb{Q}
is symmetric. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-2\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\leq\{\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}},q)\}+\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q)+(\lambda+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}})\eta^{2})\}

\displaystyle=(\lambda+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}})\eta^{2}.

Second Step

Define
q_{w}=C_{\xi}\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})
. Suppose
\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|\geq\eta
, and let
\kappa=\eta/\{2\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}\}\in[0,0.5]
. Then, noting
\mathbb{Q}
is star-convex and
C_{\xi}\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\subset\mathbb{Q}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-2\lambda\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-2\kappa^{2}\lambda\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s,a)-\gamma q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}.

Here, we use
\kappa q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\in\mathbb{Q}
. Then, from
Eq.

20
,

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\|-q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s,a)+\gamma q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\{1.5\|-q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s,a)+\gamma q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}+\eta^{2}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\gamma C_{m}C_{\eta})\eta^{2}.

Here, we use

\|-q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s,a)+\gamma q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}\leq(1+C_{m}C_{\eta})\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|^{2}_{2},

which will be explained soon. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-2\lambda\{\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-2\lambda(1+\gamma C_{m}C_{\eta})\eta^{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}(w;q_{w})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{w})=\mathbb{E}_{n}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(-q_{w}(s,a)+\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))].

Therefore, from Lemma
F.2
, noting
\eta
upper bounds the critical radius of
\mathcal{G}_{w2}
,
regarding
l(a_{1},a_{2})=a_{1},a_{1}=(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(q_{w}(s,a)-\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))
, for any
w\in\mathbb{W}
, we have

\displaystyle|\Xi_{n}(w;q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-\{\Xi(w;q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)\}|

\displaystyle\leq(\eta\mathbb{E}[\{(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(q_{w}(s,a)-\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))\}^{2}]^{1/2}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim(\eta\mathbb{E}[\{q_{w}(s,a)-\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}^{2}]^{1/2}C_{\mathbb{W}}+\eta^{2})=(\eta\|\{q_{w}(s,a)-\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}\|_{2}C_{\mathbb{W}}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\leq(\eta\{\|q_{w}(s,a)\|_{2}+\gamma\|q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|_{2}\}C_{\mathbb{W}}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle=(\eta\{\|q_{w}(s,a)\|_{2}+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}}\|q_{w}(s,a)\|_{2}\}C_{\mathbb{W}}+\eta^{2}).

We use

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[q^{2}_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]=\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{\mathrm{e}}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{w}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]^{2}]\leq\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{\mathrm{e}}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q^{2}_{w}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]]

(Jensen)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{S^{\prime}}(s),a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{\mathrm{e}}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q^{2}_{w}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{s\sim p_{S}(s),a\sim\pi^{\mathrm{b}}(a|s)}[q^{2}_{w}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]]C_{m}C_{\eta}=\|q_{w}(s,a)\|^{2}_{2}C_{m}C_{\eta}.

(
P_{S^{\prime}}(s)/P_{S}(s)\leq C_{m},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}(a|s)/\pi^{\mathrm{b}}(a|s)\leq C_{\eta}
)

Thus,

\displaystyle\kappa\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-\kappa(\eta\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})C_{\mathbb{W}}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-\kappa\eta\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})C_{\mathbb{W}}-0.5\eta^{2}

Use
\kappa\leq 0.5

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\kappa C^{-1}_{\xi}\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|^{2}_{2}-\kappa\eta\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})C_{\mathbb{W}})-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{2\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}}\{C^{-1}_{\xi}\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|^{2}_{2}-(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})C_{\mathbb{W}}\eta\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}\}-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=0.5\eta C^{-1}_{\xi}\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}-\{(0.5+0.5\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})C_{\mathbb{W}}+0.5\}\eta^{2}.

Regarding (a), we use

\displaystyle\Xi(w;q_{w})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{w})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(-q_{w}(s,a)+\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{w}]=\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))q_{w}]

\displaystyle=C^{-1}_{\xi}\|q_{w}\|^{2}_{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-2\lambda\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-\lambda(1+\gamma C_{m}C_{\eta})\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 0.5\eta C^{-1}_{\xi}\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}-\{(0.5+0.5\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})C_{\mathbb{W}}+0.5\}\eta^{2}-\lambda(1+\gamma C_{m}C_{\eta})\eta^{2}.

Combining all results

Thus,
C_{\xi}\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}=\|q_{\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}\leq\eta
or

\displaystyle C_{\xi}\eta\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}-\{(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})C_{\mathbb{W}}+1\}\eta^{2}\lesssim(\lambda+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}})\eta^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}_{\mathrm{mil}}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\leq\left\{1+\lambda(1+\gamma C_{m}C_{\eta})+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}}+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}}C_{\mathbb{W}}+1/C_{\xi}\right\}\eta.

∎

Proof of
Theorem

6.2
.

Preliminary

Define

\displaystyle\Phi(q;w)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w(s,a)],

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w(s,a)],

\displaystyle\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\Phi_{n}(q;w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n},

\displaystyle\Phi^{\lambda}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\Phi(q;w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}.

From Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\forall w\in\mathbb{W},\,|\|w\|_{n}^{2}-\|w\|^{2}_{2}|\leq 0.5\|w\|^{2}_{2}+\eta^{2}

(22)

for our choice of
\eta:=\eta_{n}+\sqrt{c_{0}\log(c_{1}/\delta)/n}
noting
\eta_{n}
upper bounds the critical radius of
\mathbb{W}
.

First Step

By definition of
\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}
and
q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{Q}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)

(23)

From Lemma
F.2
, with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\forall w\in\mathbb{W}:|\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)|\lesssim C_{1}\{\eta\|w\|_{2}+\eta^{2}\}.

(24)

Here, we use
l(a_{1},a_{2}):=a_{1}a_{2},a_{1}=w(s,a),a_{2}=r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})
is
C_{1}
-Lipschitz with respect to
a_{1}
by defining
C_{1}=R_{\max}+(1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}
. More specifically,

\displaystyle|l(a_{1},a_{2})-l(a^{\prime}_{1},a_{2})|\lesssim C_{1}|a_{1}-a^{\prime}_{1}|.

Thus,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\right\}

Definition

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)+C_{1}\eta\|w\|_{2}+C_{1}\eta^{2}-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\right\}

From
Eq.

24

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)+C_{1}\eta\|w\|_{2}+C_{1}\eta^{2}-0.5\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}+\lambda\eta^{2}\right\}

From
Eq.

22

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)+(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}\right\}.

(25)

In the last line, we use a general inequality,
a,b>0
:

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in F}(a\|f\|-b\|f\|^{2})\leq a^{2}/4b.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)+\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\geq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}+\inf_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)+\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}+\inf_{-w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};-w)+\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}+\inf_{-w\in\mathbb{W}}\{-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)+\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}-\sup_{-w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}-\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w).

Here, we use
\mathbb{W}
is symmetric. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)\right\}+\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)+(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}.

Second Step

Define

\displaystyle w_{q}=C_{\xi}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}).

Suppose
\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}\geq\eta
, and let
\kappa\coloneqq\eta/\{2\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}\}\in[0,0.5]
. Then, noting
\mathbb{W}
is star-convex,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}\geq\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-2\kappa^{2}\lambda\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|^{2}_{2,n}.

Here, we use
\kappa w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\in\mathbb{W}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|^{2}_{2,n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\{1.5\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|^{2}_{2}+0.5\eta^{2}\}

(
Eq.

22
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}.

(Definition of
\kappa
)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}\geq\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-2\lambda\eta^{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(q,w_{q})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{q})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{q(s,a)-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)].

Therefore, from Lemma
F.2
noting
\eta
upper-bounds the critical radius of
\mathcal{G}_{q}
, for any
q\in\mathbb{Q}
,

\displaystyle|\Phi_{n}(q,w_{q})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{q})-\{\Phi(q,w_{q})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{q})\}|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a))]|

\displaystyle\leq(\eta\mathbb{E}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}^{2}w^{2}_{q}(s,a)]^{1/2}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim(\eta C_{1}\|w_{q}\|_{2}+\eta^{2}).

Here, we invoke Lemma
F.2
by treating
l(a_{1},a_{2})=a_{1},\,a_{1}=\{q(s,a)-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)\}
.

Thus,

\displaystyle\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Phi(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-\kappa(C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}+\eta^{2}).

(
\kappa\leq 0.5
)

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Phi(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-\kappa(C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2})-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{=}\kappa\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})(s,a)]-\kappa(C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2})-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{2\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|}\{C^{-1}_{\xi}\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|^{2}_{2}-C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|_{2}\}-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 0.5\eta\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}-(0.5+C_{1})\eta^{2}.

For (a), we use

\displaystyle\Phi(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s,a)\{-\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})(s,a)].

Finally,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\|w\|^{2}_{2,n}\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}})\}-2\lambda\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 0.5\eta\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}-(0.5+C_{1})\eta^{2}-2\lambda\eta^{2}.

Combining all results

Thus,
\|w_{\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}}\|\leq\eta
or

\displaystyle\eta\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}-(1+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}\lesssim(C_{1}+C_{1}^{2}/\lambda+\lambda)\eta^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\leq C^{-1}_{\zeta}\eta

or

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\lesssim(1+C_{1}+\lambda+C^{2}_{1}/\lambda)\eta.

This concludes

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}_{\mathrm{mil}}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\lesssim\left(1+C_{1}+\lambda+C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C^{-1}_{\xi}\right)\eta.

∎

Proof of Corollary
6.3
.

We use Lemma
F.4
(Dudley integral). In order to show this, we calculate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}(t;\mathcal{G}_{q}),\|\cdot\|_{n})}\mathrm{d}t.

This is upper-bounded by

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\max(V(\mathbb{W}),V(\mathbb{Q}))\log(1/t)}\mathrm{d}t\right).

To prove this, we calculate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}(t;\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W},\mathbb{Q})),\|\cdot\|_{n})}\mathrm{d}t.

Recall the empirical
L^{2}
-norm as
\|f(\cdot)\|_{n}=\{1/n\sum_{i}f(s^{i},a^{i})^{2}\}^{1/2}
,
L^{\infty}
-norm as
\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty}=\max_{i}|f(s^{i},a^{i})|
. In addition, we define
\|f(\cdot)\|^{\prime}_{n,\infty}\coloneqq\max_{i}|f(s^{\prime i})|
,
\|f(\cdot)\|^{\prime}_{n}\coloneqq\{1/n\sum_{i}f(s^{\prime i})^{2}\}^{1/2}
. Then,

\displaystyle\textstyle\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t,\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W},\mathbb{Q}),\|\cdot\|_{n})}{n}}\mathrm{d}\mu(t)

\displaystyle\textstyle\leq\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t,\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W},\mathbb{Q}),\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty})}{n}}\mathrm{d}\mu(t)

\displaystyle\textstyle\leq\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{(3(1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}\},\mathbb{W},\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty})}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{3C_{\mathbb{W}}\},\mathbb{Q},\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty})}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{3\gamma C_{\mathbb{W}}\},\mathbb{V},\|\cdot\|^{\prime}_{n,\infty})}{n}}\mathrm{d}\mu(t)

\displaystyle\textstyle\leq\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{3(1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}\},\mathbb{W},\sqrt{n}\|\cdot\|_{n})}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{(3C_{\mathbb{W}}\},\mathbb{Q},\sqrt{n}\|\cdot\|_{n})}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{3\gamma C_{\mathbb{W}}\},\mathbb{V},\sqrt{n}\|\cdot\|^{\prime}_{n})}{n}}\mathrm{d}\mu(t).

Here,
\mathbb{V}=\{q(s,\pi^{\mathrm{e}});q\in\mathbb{Q}\}
.
From the second line to the third line, we use the same argument as the proof of Lemma
F.6
:

\displaystyle\textstyle\log\mathcal{N}(t,\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W},\mathbb{Q}),\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty})

\displaystyle\textstyle\leq\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{3(1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}\},\mathbb{W},\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty})+\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{3C_{\mathbb{W}}\},\mathbb{Q},\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty})

\displaystyle+\log\mathcal{N}(t/\{3\gamma C_{\mathbb{W}}\},\mathbb{V},\|\cdot\|^{\prime}_{n,\infty}),

and
\sqrt{a+b+c}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}+\sqrt{c}
. From the third line to the fourth line, we use
\|\cdot\|_{n,\infty}\leq\sqrt{n}\|\cdot\|_{n}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}(t;\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W},\mathbb{Q})),\|\cdot\|_{n})}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\textstyle O\left(\frac{\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\max(V(\mathbb{W}),V(\mathbb{Q}))\log(1/t)}\mathrm{d}t\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\max(V(\mathbb{W}),V(\mathbb{Q}))}\frac{\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\eta\log(1/\eta)\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int^{\eta}_{0}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}(t;\mathcal{G}_{q}),\|\cdot\|_{n})}\mathrm{d}t.

is similarly upper-bounded.

Finally, from Lemma
F.5
, the critical inequality becomes

\displaystyle\sqrt{\max(V(\mathbb{W}),V(\mathbb{Q}))}\frac{\log(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\eta\log(1/\eta)\leq\eta^{2}.

This inequality is satisfied with
\eta=\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\max(V(\mathbb{W}),V(\mathbb{Q}))/n})
.
∎

Proof of Corollary
6.4
.

We use Lemma
F.5
. We consider the critical radius of
\mathcal{F}
s.t.

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}(\tau;\mathcal{F},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})=(1/\tau)^{\alpha}.

When
\alpha\leq 2
, the critical inequality becomes

\displaystyle\frac{2}{2-\alpha}n^{-1/2}\eta^{1-0.5\alpha}\leq\eta^{2}.

When
\eta=\Theta(n^{-1/(2+\alpha)})
, this is satisfied.

When
\alpha=2
, the critical inequality becomes

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\{\log(\eta)-\log(\eta^{2}/2\|\mathcal{F}\|_{\infty})\}\leq\eta^{2}.

This is satisfied when
\eta=\Theta(n^{-1/4}\log n)
.

When
\alpha\geq 2
, the critical inequality becomes

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\alpha-2}n^{-1/2}\eta^{-\alpha+2}\leq\eta^{2}.

This is satisfied when
\eta=\Theta(n^{-1/(2\alpha)})
.

We apply the above analysis to our situation using the fact that the covering number of
G_{q}
is upper-bounded as in the Lemma
F.6
.
Then, the proof is immediately concluded.
∎

Proof of
Corollary

6.5
.

We consider
q_{0}\in\mathbb{Q}
which will be specified later. From
Theorem

C.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{e}})\|_{2}=O\left(\eta+\epsilon_{n}+\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0})\|_{2}^{2}}{\eta}+\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0})\|_{2}\right),

(26)

where
\eta
is the maximum of critical radiuses of
\mathbb{W}
and
\mathcal{G}_{q}
, and
\epsilon_{n}=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\inf_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\|w-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})\|_{2}
. We will bound each term in the right hand side of (
26
).

First, from Corollary
6.3
,
\eta=\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{k_{n}/n})
. Next, we take
q_{0}
s.t.

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0})\|_{2}\leq(1+\gamma)\|q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0}\|_{\infty})=O(k^{-p/d}_{n}).

from the assumption.

Next, we show
\epsilon_{n}=O(k^{-p/d}_{n})
. To do that, we check

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[-q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]\in\Lambda^{\alpha}_{2\gamma C_{\mathbb{Q}}K}(I^{d}).

Let
\|\cdot\|_{b}
be a Hölder norm. By the assumption on the density
P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)
, we can bound the Hölder norm of
\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})]

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]\|_{b}

\displaystyle=\left\|\int_{[0,1]^{d}}q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{b}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathbb{Q}}\left\|\int_{[0,1]^{d}}P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{b}\leq C_{\mathbb{Q}}\int_{[0,1]^{d}}\|P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)\|_{b}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathbb{Q}}\int_{[0,1]^{d}}K\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}=C_{\mathbb{Q}}K.

Then, for any
q\in\mathbb{Q}
, we can take
w^{\prime}\in W^{\prime}
s.t.

\displaystyle\|w^{\prime\prime}-\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}}[-q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]\|_{\infty}=O(k^{-p/d}_{n}).

Thus, by defining
w^{\prime}=w^{\prime\prime}-(q-q_{0})
, we can take
w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}
s.t.

\displaystyle\|w^{\prime}-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})\|_{2}\leq\|w^{\prime}-\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})\|_{\infty}=O(k^{-p/d}_{n}).

We use
2\mathbb{Q}_{2}+\mathbb{W}^{\prime}_{2}\subset\mathbb{W}_{2}
. Finally, the error is

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{e}})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{k_{n}/n}+k^{-p/d}_{n}+\frac{k^{-2p/d}_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}/n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\widetilde{O}(n^{-p/(2p+d)}).

(Taking balance of both terms)

∎

Proof of Theorem
6.6
.

We consider
q_{0}\in\mathbb{Q}
which will be specified later. From
Theorem

C.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{e}})\|_{2}=O\left(\eta+\epsilon_{n}+\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0})\|_{2}^{2}}{\eta}+\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0})\|_{2}\right),

(27)

where
\eta
is the maximum of critical radiuses of
\mathbb{W}
and
\mathcal{G}_{q}
, and
\epsilon_{n}=\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\inf_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\|w-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})\|_{2}
. We will bound each term in the right hand side of (
27
).

For the third and fourth terms, we consider an approximation power by fixing
q_{0}
. We set
q_{0}=\argmin_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\|q-q_{\pi^{e}}\|_{\infty}
. By Lemma
F.8
and boundedness of
\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0})\|_{2}\leq(1+\gamma)\|q_{\pi^{e}}-q_{0}\|_{\infty}=\widetilde{O}(\Xi^{-\alpha/d}).

By this result, we bound the third and fourth terms in (
27
) by

\displaystyle\widetilde{O}(\eta^{-1}\Xi^{-2\alpha/d}+\Xi^{-\alpha/d}).

About the second term
\epsilon_{n}
in (
27
), we fix
q\in\mathbb{Q}
arbitrary and consider an explicit form of
\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})=\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})-q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})]-(q-q_{0}).

We define
\mathbb{W}^{\prime}
as a set of neural network with
L
layers and
W
parameters.
By the assumption,
\mathbb{W}^{\prime}\supseteq\mathbb{Q}
holds, hence
q,q_{0}\in\mathbb{W}^{\prime}
is satisfied.
Let
\|\cdot\|_{b}
be a Sobolev norm. By the assumption on the density
P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)
, we can bound the Sobolev norm of
\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})]
as

\displaystyle\|E_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]\|_{b}

\displaystyle=\left\|\int_{[0,1]^{d}}q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{b}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathbb{Q}}\left\|\int_{[0,1]^{d}}P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{b}\leq C_{\mathbb{Q}}\int_{[0,1]^{d}}\|P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)\|_{b}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathbb{Q}}\int_{[0,1]^{d}}K\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}=C_{\mathbb{Q}}K.

Thus,

\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})-q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})]\in\mathcal{W}_{2\gamma C_{\mathbb{Q}}K}^{\alpha}(I_{d})

Hence, we pick
w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}^{\prime}
as

\displaystyle\|w^{\prime}-\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})-q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})]\|_{\infty}=\widetilde{O}(\Xi^{-\alpha/d}),

by Lemma
F.8
.
We set
w\in\mathbb{W}
as
w=w^{\prime}-q-q_{0}
and obtain

\displaystyle\|w-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|w^{\prime}-\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})-q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})]\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|w^{\prime}-\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{S^{\prime}|S,A}(s^{\prime}|\cdot)}[q(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})-q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{e})]\|_{\infty}=\widetilde{O}(\Xi^{-\alpha/d}).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\epsilon_{n}=\widetilde{O}(\Xi^{-\alpha/d}).

Combining the results with (
27
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{e}})\|_{2}\lesssim(1+C_{1})^{2}\eta+\widetilde{O}(\eta^{-1}\Xi^{-2\alpha/d}+\Xi^{-\alpha/d}).

(28)

Next, We derive an explicit order of
\eta
by deriving a critical radius of
\mathbb{W}
.
By Lemma
F.7
, we have

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon,\mathbb{W},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})\leq\Xi\log\left(\frac{2(L+1)B^{(L+1)}(3W+1)^{(L+1)}}{\varepsilon}\right)\lesssim\Xi L\log\left(\frac{LB\Xi}{\varepsilon}\right).

To the end, we derive the following local Rademacher complexity of the star-hull of
\mathbb{W}
as Lemma
F.2
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{0}^{\delta}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}(t,\mathrm{star}(\mathbb{W}),\|\cdot\|_{n})}dt

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{0}^{\delta}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}(t,\mathrm{star}(\mathbb{W}),\|\cdot\|_{\infty})}dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{0}^{\delta}\sqrt{\log(1/t)+\log\mathcal{N}(t/2,\mathbb{W},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})}dt

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{0}^{\delta}\sqrt{\log(1/t)}dt+\sqrt{\frac{\Xi L}{n}}\int_{0}^{\delta}\sqrt{\log(LB\Xi/t)}dt

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\Xi L}{n}}\delta\log(LB\Xi/\delta))\right),

for any
\delta>0
.
The second inequality follows Lemma 4.5 in
Mendelson 2002
for a covering number of a star-hull of sets.
We note that the lemma in
Mendelson 2002
holds regardless of the choice of norms.
By setting

\displaystyle\eta=\widetilde{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\Xi L}{n}}\right),

the critical inequality of
\mathbb{W}
is satisfied. Similarly, the critical inequality of
\mathcal{G}_{q}
is satisfied for this
\eta
utilizing Lemma
F.6
.

We substitute
\eta
into (
28
) and achieve

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{e}})\|_{2}\lesssim\widetilde{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\Xi L}{n}}\right)+\widetilde{O}(n^{1/2}L^{1/2}\Xi^{-2\alpha/d-1/2}+\Xi^{-\alpha/d})

Then, by setting
L=\Theta(\log n)
and
W=\Theta(n^{d/(2\alpha+d)})
, the statement is concluded.
∎

Proof of Corollary
6.7
.

The proof is done as in the proof of Corollary
5.5
.
∎

F.4
Proof of
Section

7

Proof of
Theorem

7.1
.

Recall we use sample splitting. The estimator is defined as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}[\phi(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0})]+\mathbb{E}_{n_{0}}[\phi(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{1},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{1})],

where
\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}
is the empirical average over
\mathcal{D}_{1}
and
\mathbb{E}_{n_{0}}
is the empirical average over
\mathcal{D}_{0}
. We define
\phi(s,a,r,s^{\prime};w,q)=w(s,a)(r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))+\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]
. Then, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}[\phi(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0})]-J|

\displaystyle\leq|(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[\phi(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]-(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]|

(29)

\displaystyle+|\mathbb{E}[\phi(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0})-\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]|

(30)

\displaystyle+|\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}[\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]-J|.

(31)

Recall with probability
1-2\delta^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\lesssim\eta_{w,n}\times C_{\iota,n}C^{*}_{w,n},\,\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\lesssim\eta_{w,n}\times C^{*}_{w,n},

\displaystyle\|\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\lesssim\eta_{q,n}\times C_{\iota,n}C^{*}_{q,n},\,\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\lesssim\eta_{q,n}\times C^{*}_{q,n}.

First Term

We analyze the first term (
29
). For simplicity, we write
\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0}
as
\widehat{w},\widehat{q}
.

\displaystyle(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})-\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]

\displaystyle=(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[(\widehat{w}(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(-\widehat{q}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime}))|\mathcal{D}_{0}]

(32)

\displaystyle+(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[(\widehat{w}(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(r-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime}))|\mathcal{D}_{0}]

(33)

\displaystyle+(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime})\}|\mathcal{D}_{0}].

(34)

From Berstein’s inequality, with probability
1-\delta^{\prime}
, regarding the term
Eq.

32
(we can do since
\widehat{w}
only depend on
\mathcal{D}_{0}
), we have

\displaystyle(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[(\widehat{w}(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(-\widehat{q}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime}))|\mathcal{D}_{0}]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\frac{\mathrm{var}[(\widehat{w}(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(-\widehat{q}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime}))]\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n_{1}}}+\frac{2C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{3n_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{2\frac{\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}\{C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\}^{2}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n_{1}}}+\frac{2C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{3n_{1}}.

The second term (
33
) is similarly upper-bounded. With probability
1-\delta^{\prime}
, The third term (
34
) is upper-bounded as follows:

\displaystyle(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime})\}|\mathcal{D}_{0}]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2\mathrm{var}[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime})\}]\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n_{1}}}+\frac{2C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{3n_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}(1+C_{\eta}C_{m})\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n_{1}}}+\frac{2C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{3n_{1}}.

We use

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime})\}|\mathcal{D}_{0}]

\displaystyle\lesssim C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}\{\mathrm{var}[-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)]+\mathrm{var}[\gamma\widehat{v}(s^{\prime})-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime})]\}

\displaystyle\lesssim C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}\{\mathrm{var}[-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)]+C_{\eta}C_{m}\mathrm{var}[-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)]\}.

Finally, by combining the all arguments, with probability
1-3\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle|(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})-\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\{\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}\{C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\}^{2}+\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}(1+C_{\eta}C_{m})\}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n_{1}}}+\frac{2C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{3n_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(C^{*}_{w,n}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}C_{\iota,n}\eta_{w}+C^{*}_{q,n}C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\iota,n}\eta_{q,n}\sqrt{1+C_{\eta}C_{m}})\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n_{1}}}+\frac{C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n_{1}}

Second Term

We analyze the second term (
30
). For simplicity, we write
\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0}
as
\widehat{w},\widehat{q}
. Then,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})-\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|=|\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\leq\eta_{w,n}\eta_{q,n}C^{*}_{w,n}C^{*}_{q,n}C_{\iota,n}.

Here, we use

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})-\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}(s,a)\{r-\widehat{q}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[\widehat{q}(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{r-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})].

Combining All terms

With probability
1-(\delta+10\delta^{\prime})
(From
2\times 2\delta^{\prime}+2\times 3\delta^{\prime}+\delta
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}[\phi(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{0})]+\mathbb{E}_{n_{0}}[\phi(\widehat{w}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{1},\widehat{q}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{1})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{n}[\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]-\mathbb{E}[\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]

\displaystyle+c_{0}\left\{\eta_{w,n}\eta_{q,n}C^{*}_{w,n}C^{*}_{q,n}C_{\iota,n}+(C^{*}_{w,n}\eta_{w}+C^{*}_{q,n}\eta_{q,n}\sqrt{1+C_{\eta}C_{m}})C_{\iota,n}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n}}+\frac{C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\frac{\mathrm{var}[\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]\log(1/\delta)}{n}}

\displaystyle+c_{0}\{\eta_{w,n}\eta_{q,n}C^{*}_{w,n}C^{*}_{q,n}C_{\iota,n}+(C^{*}_{w,n}\eta_{w}+C^{*}_{q,n}\eta_{q,n}\sqrt{1+C_{\eta}C_{m}})C_{\iota,n}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\mathbb{W}_{1}}C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}(\log(1/\delta^{\prime})+\log(1/\delta))}{n}\}.

for some universal constant
c_{0}
.

∎

Proof of
Theorem

7.2
.

Q-functions

Since
q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}
, for any
q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}
, there exists
\theta
s.t.
q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}=\theta^{\top}\phi
. Then,

\textstyle\mathcal{B}q-q=\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})=\theta^{\top}(\gamma\mathcal{T}-I)\phi=\theta^{\top}(\gamma M^{\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)\phi.

Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}}{\|\mathcal{B}q-q\|^{2}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\theta^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\phi(s,a)\phi^{\top}(s,a)]\theta}{\theta^{\top}(\gamma M^{\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)\mathbb{E}[\phi(s,a)\phi^{\top}(s,a)](\gamma M^{\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)^{\top}\theta}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\theta^{\top}X\theta}{\theta^{\top}(\gamma M_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)X(\gamma M_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)^{\top}\theta}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\theta^{\top}X\theta}{\theta^{\top}\bar{M}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}X(\bar{M}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})^{\top}\theta}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\max}(\{\bar{M}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}X(\bar{M}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})^{\top}\}^{-1/2}X\{M_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}X(\bar{M}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})^{\top}\}^{-1/2}).

W-functions

Since
w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\in\mathbb{W}_{1}
, for any
w\in\mathbb{W}_{1}
, there exists
\theta
s.t.
w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}=\theta^{\top}\phi
. Then,

\textstyle\mathcal{B}^{\prime}w-w=\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})=\theta^{\top}(\gamma\mathcal{T}^{\prime}-I)\phi=\theta^{\top}(\gamma M^{\prime\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)\phi.

Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{\|w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}}{\|\mathcal{B}w-w\|^{2}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\theta^{\top}X\theta}{\theta^{\top}(\gamma M^{\prime\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)X(\gamma M^{\prime\top}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)^{\top}\theta}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\theta^{\top}X\theta}{\theta^{\top}\bar{M}^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}X(\bar{M}^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})^{\top}\theta}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\max}(\{\bar{M}^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}X(\bar{M}^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})^{\top}\}^{-1/2}X\{\bar{M}^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}X(\bar{M}^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})^{\top}\}^{-1/2}).

∎

Proof of Corollary
7.3
.

Recall
Example

11
. The matrix
\mathbb{E}[\phi\phi^{\top}]
, i.e.,
X
, is defined as a matrix, whose
(i,i)
-th entry corresponding
x_{i}\in\mathcal{X}
, is
\mathbb{E}[I((s,a)=x_{i})]
, i.e.,
d_{\pi^{\mathrm{b}}}(x_{i})
. Besides,

\displaystyle\bar{M}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}

\displaystyle=(\gamma P^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)^{\top},

\displaystyle\bar{M}^{\prime}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\phi\phi^{\top}](\gamma P^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-I)\mathbb{E}[\phi\phi^{\top}]^{-1}.

All of matrices are non-singular. Thus, the assumptions in
Theorem

7.2
are satisfied.

∎

Proof of Corollary
7.4
.

Recall we use sample splitting. The estimator is defined as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]+\mathbb{E}_{n_{0}}[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})].

We define
\phi(s,a,r,s^{\prime};w,q)=w(r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))+\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]
. Then, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]-J|

\displaystyle\leq|(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]-(\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}-\mathbb{E})[\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]|

(35)

\displaystyle+|\mathbb{E}[\phi(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})-\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})|\mathcal{D}_{0}]|

(36)

\displaystyle+|\mathbb{E}_{n_{1}}[\phi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]-J|.

(37)

As in the proof of
Theorem

7.1
, it is proved that
Eqs.

35
and
36
are
\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)
. From CLT, the third term weakly converges to a normal distribution

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbb{E}[w^{2}_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{r-q(s,a)+q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}^{2}]).

From Slutsky’s theorem, the final statement is concluded.
∎

.Thus, we omit the proof.
