Proof of
Theorem

8.3
.

The main part is already proved in
(
Xie and
Jiang 2020
)
. For completeness , we prove the main part again.

Preliminary

Note that estimators for q-functions in MIL are defined as

\displaystyle\widehat{q}=\argmin_{q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Q}}\max_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(s,a,r,s;w^{\prime},q^{\prime})]|,\,f^{*}_{q}(s,a,r,s;w^{\prime},q^{\prime})=w^{\prime}(s,a)\{r-q^{\prime}(s,a)+\argmax_{a}q^{\prime}(s^{\prime},a)\}

We write this solution as
(\widehat{q},\widehat{w})
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\widehat{w}=\argmax_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(s,a,r,s;w^{\prime},\widehat{q})]|.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(s,a,r,s;w^{\prime},q^{\prime})]=\mathbb{E}[w^{\prime}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})],

where
\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}:q\to\gamma\mathbb{E}[\argmax_{a}q(s^{\prime},a)|a]-q
.

Let
\pi^{*}=\argmax_{\pi\in\Pi_{q}}J(\pi)
. Then, from
(
Xie and
Jiang 2020
, Theorem 8)
,

\displaystyle\max_{\pi\in\Pi_{q}}J(\pi)-J(\pi_{\widehat{q}})\leq 2(1-\gamma)^{-1}\max_{\pi\in\Pi_{Q}}|\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi}\{r+\gamma\max_{a}\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},a)-\widehat{q}(s,a)\}]|.

Thus,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi}\{r+\gamma\max_{a}\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},a)-\widehat{q}(s,a)\}]|

\displaystyle\leq(\max_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}[w\{r+\gamma\max_{a}\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},a)-\widehat{q}(s,a)\}]|)+\min_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}[(w_{\pi}-w^{\prime})\{r+\gamma\max_{a}\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},a)-\widehat{q}(s,a)\}]|

\displaystyle\leq(\max_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}[w\{r+\gamma\max_{a}\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},a)-\widehat{q}(s,a)\}]|)+\max_{q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Q}}\min_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}[(w_{\pi}-w^{\prime})\{r+\gamma\max_{a}q^{\prime}(s^{\prime},a)-q^{\prime}(s,a)\}]|

Here, the second term is
0
as follows since
w_{\pi}\in\mathbb{W}
. Thus,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi}\{r+\gamma\max_{a}\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},a)-\widehat{q}(s,a)\}]|\leq\max_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}[w^{\prime}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|=\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|,

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}=\argmax_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W}}|\mathbb{E}[w\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|.

As a first step, we calculate
|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|
:

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|=|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|=|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W},q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Q}}(\mathbb{E}-\mathbb{E}_{n})[|f^{*}_{q}(q^{\prime},w^{\prime})|]

Here, we use
|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|\leq|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|
and
|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{*})]|
=0

Second, we bound the difference of
|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|
and
|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|-|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|

\displaystyle\leq|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W},q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Q}}(\mathbb{E}-\mathbb{E}_{n})[|f^{*}_{q}(q^{\prime},w^{\prime})|].

Here, we use
|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|\leq|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{*}_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|
. In the end,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{*})]||\leq 4\sup_{w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{W},q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Q}}(\mathbb{E}-\mathbb{E}_{n})[|f^{*}_{q}(q^{\prime},w^{\prime})|].

Proof of
Theorem

8.4
.

The proof is similarly conducted as
Theorem

6.2
. Thus, we omit the proof.
∎

Proof of Corollary
8.5
.

From
(
Xie and
Jiang 2020
, Theorem 8)
,

\displaystyle\max_{\pi\in\Pi_{q}}J(\pi)-J(\pi_{\widehat{q}})

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\gamma)^{-1}\max_{\pi\in\Pi_{Q}}|\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi}\{r+\gamma\max_{a}\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},a)-\widehat{q}(s,a)\}]|.

Then, this implies

\displaystyle\max_{\pi\in\Pi_{q}}J(\pi)-J(\pi_{\widehat{q}})

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\gamma)^{-1}\max_{\pi\in\Pi_{Q}}|\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi}(\mathcal{B}^{*}\widehat{q}-\widehat{q})]|

\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\gamma)^{-1}\max_{\pi\in\Pi_{Q}}\|w_{\pi}\|_{2}\|\mathcal{B}^{*}\widehat{q}-\widehat{q}\|_{2}.

(CS inequality)

By the combining the statement in
Theorem

8.3
, the statement is proved.

∎

∎

F.6
Proof of
Appendix

C

Proof of
Theorem

C.1
.

We prove the case of
\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{q}
. The other case is similarly proved. We use the notation in
Theorem

6.2
. Recall the proof of
Theorem

6.2
. We have

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{q}-J|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|+\min_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|+\min_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}\max_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|.

and

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(s,a,r,s^{\prime};w,q)]=\mathbb{E}[w\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})].

Thus,

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{q}-J|\leq\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|+\min_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}\max_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|,

where

\displaystyle\textstyle\widetilde{w}=\argmax_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[w\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|.

Next, we define

\displaystyle q^{\dagger}=\argmin_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\max_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[w\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|.

As a first step, we bound
|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q^{\dagger})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|
:

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|=|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|=|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|

\displaystyle\leq 2|\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2},q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}(\mathbb{E}-\mathbb{E}_{n})[f_{q}(q,w)]|

Here, we use
|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|\leq|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|
.

Second, we bound the difference of
|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|
and
|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|
:

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|-|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|

\displaystyle\leq|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|+|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2},q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}2|(\mathbb{E}-\mathbb{E}_{n})[f_{q}(q,w)]|.

Here, we use
|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widetilde{w},\widehat{q})]|\leq|\mathbb{E}_{n}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},\widehat{q})]|
. In the end,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|

\displaystyle\leq|\mathbb{E}[\widetilde{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|-|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|+|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|-|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|+|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2},q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}4|(\mathbb{E}-\mathbb{E}_{n})[f_{q}(q,w)]|

\displaystyle+\min_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\max_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|.

Noting the first term is upper-bounded by
\mathcal{R}_{n}(\mathbb{W}_{2})R_{\max}+\mathcal{R}_{n}(\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W}_{2},\mathbb{Q}_{2}))+C_{\mathbb{W}_{2}}\{R_{\max}+C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\}\sqrt{\log(2/\delta)/n}
, the error is upper-bounded by

\displaystyle|\widehat{J}^{\mathrm{mil}}_{q}-J|

\displaystyle\leq 4\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2},q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}|(\mathbb{E}-\mathbb{E}_{n})[f_{q}(q,w)]|+|\mathbb{E}[f_{q}(\widehat{w},q^{\dagger})]|+\min_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}\max_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{n}(\mathbb{W}_{2})R_{\max}+\mathcal{R}_{n}(\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W}_{2},\mathbb{Q}_{2}))+C_{\mathbb{W}_{2}}\{R_{\max}+C_{\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\}\sqrt{\log(2/\delta)/n}+

\displaystyle+\min_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}\max_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[w\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|+\min_{w\in\mathbb{W}_{2}}\max_{q\in\mathbb{Q}_{2}}|\mathbb{E}[(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})]|.

∎

Proof of
Theorem

C.2
.

Preliminary

Define

\displaystyle\Phi(q;w)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w(s,a)],

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w(s,a)],

\displaystyle\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\Phi_{n}(q;w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n},

\displaystyle\Phi^{\lambda}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\Phi(q;w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}.

From Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\forall w\in\mathbb{W},\,|\|w\|_{n}^{2}-\|w\|^{2}_{2}|\leq 0.5\|w\|^{2}_{2}+\eta^{2}

(38)

for our choice of
\eta:=\eta_{n}+\sqrt{c_{0}\log(c_{1}/\delta)/n}
noting
\eta_{n}
upper bounds the critical radius of
\mathcal{W}
.

First Step

By definition of
\widehat{q}
and
q_{0}\in\mathbb{Q}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{q};w)\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{0};w)

(39)

From Lemma
F.2
, with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\forall w\in\mathbb{W}:|\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-\Phi(q_{0};w)|\lesssim(C_{1}+1)\{\eta\|w\|_{2}+\eta^{2}\}.

(40)

Here, we use
l(a_{1},a_{2}):=a_{1}a_{2},a_{1}=w(s,a),a_{2}=r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})
is
C_{1}
-Lipschitz with respect to
a_{1}
by defining
C_{1}=R_{\max}+(1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}
. More specifically,

\displaystyle|l(a_{1},a_{2})-l(a^{\prime}_{1},a_{2})|\lesssim C_{1}|a_{1}-a^{\prime}_{1}|.

Thus,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{0};w)

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\right\}

definition

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{0};w)+C_{1}\eta\|w\|_{2}+C_{1}\eta^{2}-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\right\}

From
Eq.

40

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{0};w)+C_{1}\eta\|w\|_{2}+C_{1}\eta^{2}-0.5\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}+\lambda\eta^{2}\right\}

From
Eq.

42

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{0};w)-0.25\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}+(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}\right\}.

(41)

In the last line, we use a general inequality,
a,b>0
:

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in F}(a\|f\|-b\|f\|^{2})\leq a^{2}/4b.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{q};w)\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)+\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle\geq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}+\inf_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)+\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}+\inf_{-w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(q_{0};-w)+\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}+\inf_{-w\in\mathbb{W}}\{-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)+\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}-\sup_{-w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}-\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{0};w).

Here, we use
\mathbb{W}
is symmetric.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{0};w)\right\}+\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(\widehat{q};w)\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q_{0};w)

(
Eq.

39
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\Phi(q_{0};w)-0.25\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}+(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}\right\}

(
Eq.

41
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\left\{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})\|_{2}\|w\|_{2}-0.25\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}+(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\lesssim}\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})\|^{2}_{2}/\lambda+(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}.

For (a), we use

\displaystyle\Phi(q_{0};w)=|\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})w]|\leq\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})\|_{2}C_{\mathbb{W}}.

Second Step

\displaystyle w_{q}=\argmin_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\|w-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})\|^{2}_{2}.

Suppose
\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}\geq\eta
, and let
\kappa\coloneqq\eta/\{2\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}\}\in[0,0.5]
. Then, noting
\mathbb{W}
is star-convex,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}\geq\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{0},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-2\kappa^{2}\lambda\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{n}.

Here, we use
\kappa w_{\widehat{q}}\in\mathbb{W}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\{1.5\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{2}+0.5\eta^{2}\}

From
Eq.

42

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}.

From definition of
\kappa

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{0};w)-2\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}\geq\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{0},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-2\lambda\eta^{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(q,w_{q})-\Phi_{n}(q_{0},w_{q})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{q(s,a)-q_{0}(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+\gamma q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)].

Therefore, from Lemma
F.2
noting
\eta
upper-bounds the critical radius of
\mathcal{G}_{q}
, for any
q\in\mathbb{Q}
,

\displaystyle|\Phi_{n}(q,w_{q})-\Phi_{n}(q_{0},w_{q})-\{\Phi(q,w_{q})-\Phi(q_{0},w_{q})\}|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{0}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{0}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a))]|

\displaystyle\leq(\eta\mathbb{E}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{0}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}^{2}w^{2}_{q}(s,a)]^{1/2}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim(\eta C_{1}\|w_{q}\|_{2}+\eta^{2}).

Here, we invoke Lemma
F.2
by treating
l(a_{1},a_{2})=a_{1},\,a_{1}=\{q(s,a)-q_{0}(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+\gamma q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}\{\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q-q_{0})(s,a)\}
.

Thus,

\displaystyle\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{0},w_{\widehat{q}})\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Phi(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi(q_{0},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-\kappa(C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}+\eta^{2}).

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Phi(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi(q_{0},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-\kappa(C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2})-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\kappa\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})(s,a)]-\kappa(C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2})-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{2\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|}\{\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})(s,a)]-C_{1}\eta\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}\}-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\geq}0.5\eta\{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}-2\epsilon_{n}\}-(0.5+C_{1})\eta^{2}.

For (a), we use

\displaystyle\Phi(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi(q_{0},w_{\widehat{q}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\{-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{0}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{0}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})(s,a)].

For (b), we use

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})(s,a)]}{\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\{-w_{\widehat{q}}+w_{\widehat{q}}+\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\}(s,a)]}{\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{2}-\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}\|w_{\widehat{q}}-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}}{\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}-\|w_{\widehat{q}}-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|_{2}-\epsilon_{n}\geq\|w_{\widehat{q}}-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})+\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}-\epsilon_{n}

\displaystyle\geq\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}-2\epsilon_{n}.

Combining all results

Thus,
\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|\leq\eta
or

\displaystyle\eta\{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}-\epsilon_{n}\}-(1+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}\lesssim\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})\|^{2}_{2}}{\lambda}+(C_{1}+C_{1}^{2}/\lambda+\lambda)\eta^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|w_{\widehat{q}}-\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}+\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\eta+\epsilon_{n}.

or

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{0})\|_{2}\lesssim(1+C_{1}+\lambda+C^{2}_{1}/\lambda)\eta+\epsilon_{n}+\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})\|^{2}_{2}}{\lambda\eta}.

In the end, from a triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}\lesssim(1+C_{1}+\lambda+C^{2}_{1}/\lambda)\eta+\epsilon_{n}+\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})\|^{2}_{2}}{\lambda\eta}+\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}-q_{0})\|_{2}.

∎

F.7
Proof of
Appendix

D

Proof of
Theorem

D.1
.

Preliminary

Define

\displaystyle\Phi(q;w)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w(s,a)],

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w(s,a)],

\displaystyle\Phi^{\lambda}_{n}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\Phi_{n}(q;w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n},

\displaystyle\Phi^{\lambda}(q;w)

\displaystyle=\Phi(q;w)-\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{2}.

From Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\forall w\in\mathbb{W},\,|\|w\|_{n}^{2}-\|w\|^{2}_{2}|\leq 0.5\|w\|^{2}_{2}+\eta^{2}

(42)

for our choice of
\eta:=\eta_{n}+\sqrt{c_{0}\log(c_{1}/\delta)/n}
noting
\eta_{n}
upper bounds the critical radius of
\mathbb{W}
.

First Step

First step is conducted as in the proof of
Theorem

6.2
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim(C^{2}_{1}/\lambda+C_{1}+\lambda)\eta^{2}.

Second Step

Given
q
,we define
w_{q}
s.t.

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}w_{q}=C_{\xi}(q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}).

Suppose
\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\geq C_{\xi}\eta
, and let
\kappa\coloneqq\eta/\{2C_{\xi}\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\}\in[0,0.5]
. Then, noting
\mathbb{W}
is star-convex,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\lambda\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}\geq\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-2\kappa^{2}\lambda\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{n}.

Here, we use
\kappa w_{\widehat{q}}\in\mathbb{W}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\{1.5\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{2}+0.5\eta^{2}\}

(
Eq.

42
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}\frac{\|w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{2}}{\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}w_{\widehat{q}}\|^{2}_{2}}+\eta^{2}.

(Definition of
\kappa
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}C^{2}_{\iota}+\eta^{2}.

(Definition of
C_{\iota}
)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{w\in\mathbb{W}}\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q};w)-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};w)-2\|w\|^{2}_{n}\}\geq\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-2\lambda\{1+C^{2}_{\iota}\}\eta^{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(q,w_{q})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{q})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{q(s,a)-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)].

Therefore, from Lemma
F.2
noting
\eta
upper-bounds the critical radius of
\mathcal{G}_{q}
, for any
q\in\mathbb{Q}
,

\displaystyle|\Phi_{n}(q,w_{q})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{q})-\{\Phi(q,w_{q})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{q})\}|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a))]|

\displaystyle\leq(\eta\mathbb{E}[\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}^{2}w^{2}_{q}(s,a)]^{1/2}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}+\eta^{2}.

Here, we invoke Lemma
F.2
by treating
l(a_{1},a_{2})=a_{1},\,a_{1}=\{q(s,a)-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}w_{q}(s,a)\}
.

Thus,

\displaystyle\kappa\{\Phi_{n}(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi_{n}(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}})\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Phi(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-\kappa(\eta C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}+\eta^{2}).

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Phi(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}})\}-\kappa\eta C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\kappa\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})(s,a)]-\kappa\eta C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{2C_{\xi}\|(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}}\{C_{\xi}\|(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|^{2}_{2}-\eta C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})\|q-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\}-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 0.5\eta\|(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})\|_{2}-(0.5+C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}}))\eta^{2}.

From the second line to the third line, we use

\displaystyle\Phi(\widehat{q},w_{\widehat{q}})-\Phi(q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},w_{\widehat{q}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\{-\widehat{q}(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma\widehat{q}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})-\gamma q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})(s,a)]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{T}^{\prime}_{\gamma}w_{\widehat{q}}(s,a)(\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})(s,a)]=C_{\xi}\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}.

Combining all results

Thus,
C_{\xi}\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\leq\eta
or

\displaystyle\eta\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}-(1+C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}}))\eta^{2}-\lambda(1+C^{2}_{\iota})\eta^{2}\lesssim(C_{1}+C_{1}^{2}/\lambda+\lambda)\eta^{2}.

In the end,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{q}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+C_{1}+C_{1}^{2}/\lambda+\lambda\{1+C^{2}_{\iota}\}+C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma\sqrt{C_{m}C_{\eta}})+1/C_{\xi})\eta\,

\displaystyle C_{1}

\displaystyle=C_{\mathbb{Q}}+(1-\gamma^{-1})R_{\max}.

∎

Proof of
Theorem

D.2
.

Preliminary

Define

\displaystyle\Xi(w,q)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)\{-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]],

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}(w,q)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{n}[w(s,a)\{-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\}+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]],

\displaystyle\Xi^{\lambda}(w,q)

\displaystyle=\Xi^{\lambda}(w,q)-\lambda\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w,q)

\displaystyle=\Xi^{\lambda}_{n}(w,q)-\lambda\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{n}.

From Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\forall q\in\mathbb{Q},|\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{n}-\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}|\leq 0.5\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}+0.5\eta^{2}.

(43)

for our choice of
\eta:=\eta_{n}+c_{0}\sqrt{\log(c_{1}/\delta)/n}
noting
\eta_{n}
upper bounds the critical of
\mathcal{G}_{w1}
.

First Step

As in the proof of
Theorem

6.1
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-2\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle\leq(\lambda+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}})\eta^{2}.

Second Step

Given
w
, we define
q_{w}\in\mathbb{Q}
s.t.
\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{w}\coloneqq C_{\xi}(w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})
noting
C_{\xi}\{\mathbb{W}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\}\subset\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}\mathbb{Q}
. Suppose
C_{\xi}\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\geq\eta
, and let
\kappa\coloneqq\eta/\{2C_{\xi}\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\}\in[0,0.5]
. Then, noting
\mathbb{Q}
is star-convex,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-2\lambda\|q(s,a)-\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{n}\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w};q_{\widehat{w}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}})\}-2\kappa^{2}\lambda\|q_{\widehat{w}}(s,a)-\gamma q_{\widehat{w}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{n}.

Here, we use
\kappa q_{\widehat{w}}\in\mathbb{Q}
, and take the value of
\kappa q_{\widehat{w}}
. Then, from
Eq.

43
,

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\|-q_{\widehat{w}}(s,a)+\gamma q_{\widehat{w}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\{1.5\|-q_{\widehat{w}}(s,a)+\gamma q_{\widehat{w}}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{2}+\eta^{2}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}(1+C_{m}C_{\eta})\|q_{\widehat{w}}\|^{2}_{2}+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta^{2}}{\|\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{\widehat{w}}\|^{2}_{2}}(1+C_{m}C_{\eta})\|q_{\widehat{w}}\|^{2}_{2}+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta^{2}(1+C_{m}C_{\eta})C^{2}_{\iota}+\eta^{2}

(Definition of
C_{\iota}
)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{q\in\mathbb{Q}}\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w};q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-2\lambda\|-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\|^{2}_{n}\}\geq\kappa(\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w};q_{\widehat{w}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}}))-2\lambda\eta^{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}(w;q_{w})-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{w})=\mathbb{E}_{n}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(-q_{w}(s,a)+\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))].

Therefore, from Lemma
F.2
, noting
\eta
upper bounds the critical radius of
\mathcal{G}_{w2}
,
regarding
l(a_{1},a_{2})=a_{1},a_{1}=(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(q_{w}(s,a)-\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))
,
\forall w\in\mathbb{W}
,

\displaystyle|\Xi_{n}(w;q)-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)-\{\Xi(w;q)-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q)\}|

\displaystyle\leq(\eta\mathbb{E}[\{(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(q_{w}(s,a)-\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))\}^{2}]^{1/2}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\leq(\eta\mathbb{E}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))^{2}]^{1/2}C_{\mathbb{Q}}+\eta^{2}).

Here, we use
\|q_{w}(s,a)-\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))\|_{\infty}\lesssim C_{\mathbb{Q}}
.

Thus,

\displaystyle\kappa\{\Xi_{n}(\widehat{w};q_{\widehat{w}})-\Xi_{n}(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}})\}

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi(\widehat{w};q_{\widehat{w}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}})\}-\kappa(\eta\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}C_{\mathbb{Q}}+\eta^{2})

\displaystyle\geq\kappa\{\Xi(\widehat{w};q_{\widehat{w}})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{\widehat{w}})\}-\kappa(\eta\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}C_{\mathbb{Q}})-0.5\eta^{2}

Use
\kappa\leq 0.5

\displaystyle=rC_{\xi}\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}-\kappa(\eta\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}C_{\mathbb{Q}})-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{2C_{\xi}\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}}\{C_{\xi}\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}-C_{\mathbb{Q}}\eta\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\}-0.5\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=0.5\eta\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}-(0.5+0.5C_{\mathbb{Q}})\eta^{2}.

From the third line to the fourth line, we use

\displaystyle\Xi(w;q_{w})-\Xi(w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}};q_{w})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(-q_{w}(s,a)+\gamma q_{w}(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))\mathcal{T}_{\gamma}q_{w}]

\displaystyle=C_{\xi}\mathbb{E}[(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))(w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a))]=C_{\xi}\|w-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|^{2}_{2}.

Combining all results

Thus,
C_{\xi}\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\leq\eta
or

\displaystyle\eta\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}-(1+C_{\mathbb{Q}})\eta^{2}-\lambda\eta^{2}C^{2}_{\iota}(1+C_{m}C_{\eta})\lesssim(\lambda+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}})\eta^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{w}-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\leq\left\{1+C^{2}_{\mathbb{W}}/\lambda+C_{\mathbb{W}}+\lambda\{1+C^{2}_{\iota}(1+C_{m}C_{\eta})\}+C_{\mathbb{Q}}+1/C_{\xi}\right\}\eta.

∎

Appendix

Proof of Lemma
E.2
.

We define

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(q)=\mathbb{E}_{\zeta_{k}}[\{r+\gamma f_{k-1}(s^{\prime})-q(s,a)\}^{2}-\{r+\gamma f_{k-1}(s^{\prime})-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}(s,a)\}^{2}].

From the definition of the estimator and the assumption
\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}\in\mathbb{Q}
using
\mathcal{B}\mathbb{Q}\subset\mathbb{Q}
,

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(f_{k})\leq 0.

Note

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\zeta_{k}}[\{r+\gamma f_{k-1}(s^{\prime})-q(s,a)\}^{2}-\{r+\gamma f_{k-1}(s^{\prime})-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}(s,a)\}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\zeta_{k}}[\{-q(s,a)+\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}(s,a)\}\{2r+2\gamma f_{k-1}(s^{\prime})-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}(s,a)-q(s,a)\}].

By Lemma
F.2
,
\forall q\in\mathbb{Q}
,

\displaystyle|\Phi_{n}(q)-\Phi(q)\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta(\{\mathbb{E}[\{-q(s,a)+\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}(s,a)\}^{2}\{2r+2\gamma f_{k-1}(s^{\prime})-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}(s,a)-q(s,a)\}^{2}]\}^{1/2}+\eta)

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta(\{R_{\max}+C_{\mathbb{Q}}\}\|q-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}\|_{2}+\eta).

We have

\displaystyle 0\geq\Phi_{n}(f_{k})

\displaystyle=\Phi_{n}(f_{k})-\Phi(f_{k})+\Phi(f_{k})

\displaystyle\geq\Phi(f_{k})-\eta(\{R_{\max}+C_{\mathbb{Q}}\}\|f_{k}-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}\|_{2}+\eta)

\displaystyle=\|f_{k}-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}\|^{2}_{2}-\eta(\{R_{\max}+C_{\mathbb{Q}}\}\|f_{k}-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}\|_{2}+\eta).

This implies
\|f_{k}-\mathcal{B}f_{k-1}\|_{2}\lesssim(R_{\max}+C_{\mathbb{Q}})\eta
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
E.3
.

We define
\{\mathbb{E}_{\mu}[f(\cdot)^{q}]\}^{1/q}=\|\cdot\|_{q,\mu}
.

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{0}}[f_{K}(s,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{0}}[q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]|=

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{1,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}

(
d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}\coloneqq d_{0}\times\pi^{\mathrm{e}}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}

(Jensen)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}+\|\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}+\|\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}-\mathcal{B}q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}+\gamma\|f_{K-1}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(b)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}+\sqrt{\gamma}\|f_{K-1}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,\gamma d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},2}},

(
\star
)

where (a) is from the following argument and (b) follows from the first part of
(
Agarwal
et al. 2019
, Fact 5.1)
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}-\mathcal{B}q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}^{2}=

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}\left[\big((\mathcal{B}f_{K-1})(s,a)-(\mathcal{B}q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}})(s,a)\big)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\gamma^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim\Pr(\cdot,\cdot|s,a,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})}[f_{K-1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\gamma^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim\Pr(\cdot,\cdot|s,a,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})}\left[\left(f_{K-1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)^{2}\right]\right]

(Jensen)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\gamma^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},2}}\left[\left(f_{K-1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\gamma^{2}\|f_{K-1}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},2}}^{2},

where
\Pr(\cdot,\cdot|s,a,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})
denotes the distribution of next the state-action pair from
(s,a)
when following policy
\pi^{\mathrm{e}}
.

By applying (

⋆

‣
F.8
) inductively, we have

\displaystyle~|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{0}}[f_{K}(s,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{0}}[q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]|

(44)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{1,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}+\sqrt{\gamma}\|f_{K-1}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,\gamma d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|f_{K}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},1}}+\sqrt{\gamma}\|f_{K-1}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-2}\|_{2,\gamma d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},2}}+\gamma\|f_{K-2}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,\gamma^{2}d_{3}^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\gamma^{k/2}\|f_{K-k}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1-k}\|_{2,\gamma^{k}d_{k+1}^{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}}+\gamma^{{K}/{2}}\|f_{0}-q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2,d_{{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}},{K+1}}}.

By the definition of
d_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}
, we know
\gamma^{k}d_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}},k+1}\leq\frac{d_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}},\gamma}}{1-\gamma}
for any
k\geq 1
. Therefore, using the second part of
(
Agarwal
et al. 2019
, Fact 5.1)
, we obtain

\displaystyle~|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{0}}[f_{K}(s,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{0}}[q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\gamma}}\gamma^{k/2}\|f_{K-k}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1-k}\|_{2,d_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}},\gamma}}+\gamma^{{K}/{2}}(C_{\mathbb{Q}}+(1-\gamma)^{-1}R_{\max})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\gamma}}\gamma^{k/2}\|w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\|f_{K-k}-\mathcal{B}f_{K-1-k}\|_{2}+\gamma^{{K}/{2}}(C_{\mathbb{Q}}+(1-\gamma)^{-1}R_{\max})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\gamma}}\varepsilon\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\gamma^{k/2}+\gamma^{{K}/{2}}(C_{\mathbb{Q}}+(1-\gamma)^{-1}R_{\max})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\frac{1-\gamma^{K/2}}{\sqrt{1-\gamma}(1-\gamma^{1/2})}\|w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}\|_{2}\varepsilon+\gamma^{{K}/{2}}(C_{\mathbb{Q}}+(1-\gamma)^{-1}R_{\max}).

∎

F.9
Proof of auxiliary lemmas

Lemma F.3
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)(r-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}}))+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[q(s_{0},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})]]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\{w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\}\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma v(s^{\prime})-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s)\}]+J(\pi^{\mathrm{e}}).

Proof of Lemma
F.3
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma v(s^{\prime})\}]+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[v(s_{0})]-J(\pi^{\mathrm{e}})+J(\pi^{\mathrm{e}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma v(s^{\prime})\}]+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[v(s_{0})]

\displaystyle-\{\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma v(s^{\prime})\}]+(1-\gamma)\mathbb{E}_{d_{0}}[v(s_{0})]\}+J(\pi^{\mathrm{e}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[w(s,a)\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma v(s^{\prime})\}]-\mathbb{E}[w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\{r-q(s,a)+\gamma v(s^{\prime})\}]+J(\pi^{\mathrm{e}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\{w(s,a)-w_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)\}\{-q(s,a)+q_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s,a)+\gamma v(s^{\prime})-\gamma v_{\pi^{\mathrm{e}}}(s^{\prime})\}]+J(\pi^{\mathrm{e}}).

∎

Lemma F.4

(Dudley integral)
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{n}(\mathcal{F})\lesssim\inf_{\tau\geq 0}\left\{\tau+\int_{\tau}^{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{n}[f^{2}]}}\sqrt{\frac{\log\mathcal{N}(\tau,\mathcal{F},\|\cdot\|_{n})}{n}}\right\}.

Note
\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}_{n}[f^{2}]}
is upper bounded by the envelope
\|\mathcal{F}\|_{\infty}
.

Lemma F.5
.

(
Wainwright 2019
, Corollary 14.3)

Let
\mathcal{N}(\tau;\mathbb{B}(\delta;\mathcal{F}),\|\cdot\|_{n})
denote the
\tau
-covering number of
\mathbb{B}_{n}(\delta;\mathcal{F}):=\{f|\|f\|_{n}\leq\delta\}
. Then, the critical inequality of the empirical version is satisfied for any
\delta>0
s.t.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int^{\delta}_{\delta^{2}/(2\|\mathcal{F}\|_{\infty})}\sqrt{\log\mathcal{N}(t,\mathbb{B}_{n}(\delta;\mathcal{F}),\|\cdot\|_{n})}\mathrm{d}t\leq\frac{\delta^{2}}{\|\mathcal{F}\|_{\infty}}.

We analyze the main error terms in MDL and MIL. We assume
\mathbb{W}_{2}=\mathbb{Q}_{2}=\mathbb{W}_{1}=\mathbb{W}_{2}
for simplicity. We consider a joint hypothesis set and investigate its covering number:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W},\mathbb{Q})\textstyle

\displaystyle=\textstyle\{(s,a,s^{\prime})\mapsto w(s,a)\{-q(s,a)+\gamma q(s^{\prime},\pi^{\mathrm{e}})\};w\in\mathbb{W},q\in\mathbb{Q}\}.

To analyze above, we often use the following lemma.

Lemma F.6

(Joint covering number)
.

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}(\tau,\mathcal{G}(\mathbb{W},\mathbb{Q}),\|\cdot\|_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq\log\mathcal{N}(0.5\tau/((1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}),\mathbb{W},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})+\log\mathcal{N}(0.5\tau/(C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma)),\mathbb{Q},\|\cdot\|_{\infty}).

Proof of Lemma
F.6
.

Fix
\varepsilon_{1}>0
and
\varepsilon_{2}>0
and set
N_{Q}=\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon_{1},\mathbb{Q},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})
and
N_{W}=\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon_{2},\mathbb{W},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})
.
We pick an
\varepsilon_{1}
-covering set
\{q_{i}\}_{i=1}^{N_{Q}}\subset\mathbb{Q}
, that is, for any
q\in\mathbb{Q}
, there exists
q_{i}\in\{q_{i}\}_{i=1}^{N_{Q}}
such as
\|q-q_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\varepsilon_{1}
.
Similarly, we pick an
\varepsilon_{2}
-covering set
\{w_{j}\}_{j=1}^{N_{W}}\subset\mathbb{W}
.

We consider a functional
G:\mathbb{W}\times\mathbb{Q}\to\mathcal{G}
such as

\displaystyle G[w,q](s,a,r,s^{\prime}):=w(s,a)\{-q(s,a)+\gamma\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]\}.

Simply, we have
\mathcal{G}=\{G[w,q];w\in\mathbb{W},q\in\mathbb{Q}\}
.
Using the picked covering set, we consider a finite set
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}:=\{G[w_{j},q_{i}];i=1,...,N_{Q},j=1,...,N_{W}\}\subset\mathcal{G}
.
Obviously, we have
|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}|\leq N_{W}N_{Q}
.

We will show that
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}
is a covering set of
\mathcal{G}
.
Pick
G[w,q]\in\mathcal{G}
arbitrary, then we can pick
w_{j}
and
q_{i}
such as
\|q-q_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\varepsilon_{1}
and
\|w-w_{j}\|_{\infty}\leq\varepsilon_{2}
.
Then, we evaluate
G[w_{j},q_{i}](s,a,r,s^{\prime})-G[w,q](s,a,r,s^{\prime})
as

\displaystyle|G[w_{j},q_{i}](s,a,r,s^{\prime})-G[w,q](s,a,r,s^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=|w_{j}(s,a)\{-q_{i}(s,a)+\gamma\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{i}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]\}-w(s,a)\{q(s,a)+\gamma\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]\}|

\displaystyle=|(w_{j}(s,a)-w(s,a))\{-q_{i}(s,a)+\gamma\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{i}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]\}

\displaystyle\quad+w(s,a)[-(q(s,a)-q_{i}(s,a))+\gamma(\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{i}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]-\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})])]|

\displaystyle\leq|w_{j}(s,a)-w(s,a)||-q_{i}(s,a)+\gamma\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{i}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]|

\displaystyle\quad+|w(s,a)||-(q(s,a)-q_{i}(s,a))+\gamma(\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{i}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-q(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})])|

\displaystyle=:I+II.

We bound the terms
I
and
II
separately.
For the first term
I
, we have

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq|w_{j}(s,a)-w(s,a)|(|q_{i}(s,a)|+|\gamma\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{i}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]||)

\displaystyle\leq\|w_{j}-w\|_{\infty}(\|q_{i}\|_{\infty}+\gamma\|q_{i}\|_{\infty})\leq\varepsilon_{2}(1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}.

For the second term
II
, we have

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\leq\|w\|_{\infty}(|q(s,a)-q_{i}(s,a)|+\gamma|\mathrm{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi_{e}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})}[q_{i}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-q(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})]|)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathbb{W}}(\|q-q_{i}\|_{\infty}+\gamma\|q-q_{i}\|_{\infty})\leq C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma)\varepsilon_{1}.

Combining the bound, we find that
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}
is a
(\varepsilon_{2}(1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}}+C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma)\varepsilon_{1})
-covering set of
\mathcal{G}
.
That is, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon_{2}((1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}})+C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma)\varepsilon_{1},\mathcal{G},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})\leq N_{Q}N_{W}=\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon_{1},\mathbb{Q},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})\mathcal{N}(\varepsilon_{2},\mathbb{W},\|\cdot\|_{\infty}).

Substituting
\varepsilon_{2}=0.5\varepsilon/((1+\gamma)C_{\mathbb{Q}})
and
\varepsilon_{1}=0.5\varepsilon/(C_{\mathbb{W}}(1+\gamma))
yields the statement.
∎

We consider using neutral networks with an ReLU activation function. The covering number of these neural networks is calculated as follows.

Lemma F.7

(Neural network; Lemma 21 in
Nakada and
Imaizumi 2020
)
.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{NN}
be a set of functions by a neural network with
L
layers,
\Omega
weights in
[-B,B]
, and
1-
Lipschitz continuous activation function
Then, for
\tau\in(0,1]
, we have

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}(\tau,\mathcal{F}_{NN},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})\leq\Omega\log\left(\frac{2LB^{L}(\Omega+1)^{L}}{\tau}\right).

Besides, it is known that these neural networks approximate the Sobolev ball.

Lemma F.8

(Theorem 1 in
Yarotsky 2017
)
.

Suppose
q_{0}
belongs to the Sobolev ball with an order
p
with its input is
d
-dimensional.
Then, there exists the neural network with a ReLU activation function, which has at most
L=O(\log\Omega)
layers and
\Omega
parameters s.t.

\displaystyle\|q^{*}-q_{0}\|_{\infty}=\widetilde{O}(\Omega^{-p/d}).

For a matrix
A
, let
\|A\|_{F}:=\sqrt{\sum_{i,j}A_{i,j}^{2}}
be its Frobenius norm.

Lemma F.9

(Theorem 1 in
Golowich
et al. 2018
)
.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{NN}
be a set of functions by a neural network with
L
layers and activation function which is
1
-Lipschitz continuous and positive-homogeneous, that is,
\sigma(az)=a\sigma(z)
holds with
a>0
.
Further, let
A_{\ell}
be a weight matrix of an
\ell
-th layers for
\ell=1,...,L
and assume that
\|A_{\ell}\|_{F}\leq M_{F}(\ell)
with a bound
M_{F}(\ell)
.
Then, we have
\mathcal{R}_{n}(\mathcal{F}_{NN})\leq\max_{x}\|x\|_{2}(2\sqrt{\log L}+1)\prod_{\ell=1}^{L}M_{F}(\ell)/\sqrt{n}
.
